### Functions Intermediate Code Operations (SlithIR & SSA)
#### Function: `calculateLiquidityUnits`
<details><summary>View SlithIR Operations</summary>

```
TMP_0(uint256) = INTERNAL_CALL, VaderMath.calculateSlipAdjustment(uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256)(vaderDeposited,vaderBalance,assetDeposited,assetBalance)
slip(uint256) := TMP_0(uint256)
TMP_1(uint256) = vaderBalance (c)* assetDeposited
TMP_2(uint256) = vaderDeposited (c)* assetBalance
TMP_3(uint256) = TMP_1 (c)+ TMP_2
poolUnitFactor(uint256) := TMP_3(uint256)
TMP_4(uint256) = ONE (c)* 2
TMP_5(uint256) = TMP_4 (c)* vaderBalance
TMP_6(uint256) = TMP_5 (c)* assetBalance
denominator(uint256) := TMP_6(uint256)
TMP_7(uint256) = totalPoolUnits (c)* poolUnitFactor
TMP_8(uint256) = TMP_7 (c)/ denominator
TMP_9(uint256) = TMP_8 (c)* slip
RETURN TMP_9
```
</details>
<details><summary>View SSA Operations</summary>

```
No SSA operations.
```
</details>

#### Function: `calculateSlipAdjustment`
<details><summary>View SlithIR Operations</summary>

```
TMP_10(uint256) = vaderBalance (c)* assetDeposited
vaderAsset(uint256) := TMP_10(uint256)
TMP_11(uint256) = assetBalance (c)* vaderDeposited
assetVader(uint256) := TMP_11(uint256)
TMP_12(uint256) = vaderDeposited (c)+ vaderBalance
TMP_13(uint256) = assetDeposited (c)+ assetBalance
TMP_14(uint256) = TMP_12 (c)* TMP_13
denominator(uint256) := TMP_14(uint256)
TMP_15(uint256) = INTERNAL_CALL, VaderMath.delta(uint256,uint256)(vaderAsset,assetVader)
TMP_16(uint256) = TMP_15 (c)/ denominator
TMP_17(uint256) = ONE (c)- TMP_16
RETURN TMP_17
```
</details>
<details><summary>View SSA Operations</summary>

```
No SSA operations.
```
</details>

#### Function: `calculateLoss`
<details><summary>View SlithIR Operations</summary>

```
TMP_18(uint256) = originalAsset (c)* releasedVader
TMP_19(uint256) = TMP_18 (c)/ releasedAsset
TMP_20(uint256) = TMP_19 (c)+ originalVader
originalValue(uint256) := TMP_20(uint256)
TMP_21(uint256) = releasedAsset (c)* releasedVader
TMP_22(uint256) = TMP_21 (c)/ releasedAsset
TMP_23(uint256) = TMP_22 (c)+ releasedVader
releasedValue(uint256) := TMP_23(uint256)
TMP_24(bool) = originalValue > releasedValue
CONDITION TMP_24
TMP_25(uint256) = originalValue (c)- releasedValue
loss(uint256) := TMP_25(uint256)
RETURN loss
```
</details>
<details><summary>View SSA Operations</summary>

```
No SSA operations.
```
</details>

#### Function: `calculateSwap`
<details><summary>View SlithIR Operations</summary>

```
TMP_26(uint256) = amountIn (c)* reserveIn
TMP_27(uint256) = TMP_26 (c)* reserveOut
numerator(uint256) := TMP_27(uint256)
TMP_28(uint256) = amountIn (c)+ reserveIn
TMP_29(uint256) = INTERNAL_CALL, VaderMath.pow(uint256)(TMP_28)
denominator(uint256) := TMP_29(uint256)
TMP_30(uint256) = numerator (c)/ denominator
amountOut(uint256) := TMP_30(uint256)
RETURN amountOut
```
</details>
<details><summary>View SSA Operations</summary>

```
No SSA operations.
```
</details>

#### Function: `calculateSwapReverse`
<details><summary>View SlithIR Operations</summary>

```
TMP_31(uint256) = reserveIn (c)* reserveOut
XY(uint256) := TMP_31(uint256)
TMP_32(uint256) = amountOut (c)* 2
y2(uint256) := TMP_32(uint256)
TMP_33(uint256) = y2 (c)* 2
y4(uint256) := TMP_33(uint256)
TMP_34(bool) = y4 < reserveOut
TMP_35(None) = SOLIDITY_CALL require(bool,string)(TMP_34,VaderMath::calculateSwapReverse: Desired Output Exceeds Maximum Output Possible (1/4 of Liquidity Pool))
TMP_36(uint256) = INTERNAL_CALL, VaderMath.root(uint256)(XY)
TMP_37(uint256) = reserveOut (c)- y4
TMP_38(uint256) = reserveIn (c)* TMP_37
TMP_39(uint256) = INTERNAL_CALL, VaderMath.root(uint256)(TMP_38)
TMP_40(uint256) = TMP_36 (c)* TMP_39
numeratorA(uint256) := TMP_40(uint256)
TMP_41(uint256) = y2 (c)* reserveIn
numeratorB(uint256) := TMP_41(uint256)
numeratorC(uint256) := XY(uint256)
TMP_42(uint256) = numeratorC (c)- numeratorA
TMP_43(uint256) = TMP_42 (c)- numeratorB
numerator(uint256) := TMP_43(uint256)
denominator(uint256) := y2(uint256)
TMP_44(uint256) = numerator (c)/ denominator
amountIn(uint256) := TMP_44(uint256)
RETURN amountIn
```
</details>
<details><summary>View SSA Operations</summary>

```
No SSA operations.
```
</details>

#### Function: `root`
<details><summary>View SlithIR Operations</summary>

c(uint256) := a(uint256)
TMP_50(uint256) = a (c)/ 2

x(uint256) := TMP_51(uint256)
TMP_52(bool) = x < c
CONDITION TMP_52
c(uint256) := x(uint256)
TMP_53(uint256) = a (c)/ x
TMP_54(uint256) = TMP_53 (c)+ x
TMP_55(uint256) = TMP_54 (c)/ 2
x(uint256) := TMP_55(uint256)
TMP_56(bool) = a != 0
CONDITION TMP_56
c(uint256) := 1(uint256)
RETURN c

## Contract: VaderPool
### Linearised Constructor Execution sequence
1. `ERC721.constructor(string, string)`
2. `Ownable.constructor()`
3. `ReentrancyGuard.constructor()`
4. `BasePool.constructor()`
5. `BasePool.constructor()`
6. `BasePool.constructor(string, string)`
7. `BasePool.constructor(IERC20Extended, IERC20Extended)`

### Inter-Contract & Function Call Graph (Mermaid)
```mermaid
flowchart LR
    VaderPool.constructor --> IERC20Extended.symbol
    VaderPool.mint --> VaderMath.calculateLiquidityUnits
    VaderPool.mint --> IERC20.balanceOf
    VaderPool._burn --> IERC20.balanceOf
    VaderPool._burn --> SafeERC20.safeTransfer
    VaderPool.swap --> SafeERC20.safeTransfer
    VaderPool.swap --> VaderMath.calculateSwap
    VaderPool.swap --> IERC20.balanceOf
    VaderPool._update --> UQ112x112.encode
    VaderPool._update --> UQ112x112.uqdiv
    VaderPool.tokenURI --> Strings.toString
    VaderPool._checkOnERC721Received --> IERC721Receiver.onERC721Received
    VaderPool._checkOnERC721Received --> Address.isContract
    VaderPool.burn --> VaderMath.calculateLoss
```

#### Function: `consult`
<details><summary>View SlithIR Operations</summary>

```
REF_76 -> LENGTH _pairs
pairCount(uint256) := REF_76(uint256)
sumNative(uint256) := 0(uint256)
sumUSD(uint256) := 0(uint256)
i(uint256) := 0(uint256)
TMP_396(bool) = i < pairCount
CONDITION TMP_396
REF_77(TwapOracle.PairData) -> _pairs[i]
pairData(TwapOracle.PairData) := REF_77(TwapOracle.PairData)
REF_78(address) -> pairData.token0
TMP_397(bool) = token == REF_78
CONDITION TMP_397
REF_79(FixedPoint.uq112x112) -> pairData.price1Average
TMP_398(FixedPoint.uq144x112) = LIBRARY_CALL, dest:FixedPoint, function:FixedPoint.mul(FixedPoint.uq112x112,uint256), arguments:['REF_79', '1'] 
TMP_399(uint144) = LIBRARY_CALL, dest:FixedPoint, function:FixedPoint.decode144(FixedPoint.uq144x112), arguments:['TMP_398'] 
sumNative(uint256) = sumNative (c)+ TMP_399
REF_82(FixedPoint.uq112x112) -> pairData.price1Average
REF_83(uint224) -> REF_82._x
TMP_400(bool) = REF_83 != 0
CONDITION TMP_400
TMP_401(bool) = sumNative != 0
TMP_402(None) = SOLIDITY_CALL require(bool)(TMP_401)
REF_84(address) -> pairData.token1
REF_85(address) -> _aggregators[REF_84]
TMP_403 = CONVERT REF_85 to AggregatorV3Interface
TUPLE_7(uint80,int256,uint256,uint256,uint80) = HIGH_LEVEL_CALL, dest:TMP_403(AggregatorV3Interface), function:latestRoundData, arguments:[]  
roundID(uint80)= UNPACK TUPLE_7 index: 0 
price(int256)= UNPACK TUPLE_7 index: 1 
answeredInRound(uint80)= UNPACK TUPLE_7 index: 4 
TMP_404(bool) = answeredInRound >= roundID
TMP_405(None) = SOLIDITY_CALL require(bool,string)(TMP_404,TwapOracle::consult: stale chainlink price)
TMP_406(bool) = price != 0
TMP_407(None) = SOLIDITY_CALL require(bool,string)(TMP_406,TwapOracle::consult: chainlink malfunction)
TMP_408 = CONVERT price to uint256
TMP_409(uint256) = 10 (c)** 10
TMP_410(uint256) = TMP_408 (c)* TMP_409
sumUSD(uint256) = sumUSD (c)+ TMP_410
TMP_411(uint256) := i(uint256)
i(uint256) = i (c)+ 1
TMP_412(bool) = sumNative != 0
TMP_413(None) = SOLIDITY_CALL require(bool,string)(TMP_412,TwapOracle::consult: Sum of native is zero)
TMP_414 = CONVERT token to IERC20Metadata
TMP_415(uint8) = HIGH_LEVEL_CALL, dest:TMP_414(IERC20Metadata), function:decimals, arguments:[]  
TMP_416(uint256) = sumUSD (c)* TMP_415
TMP_417(uint256) = TMP_416 (c)/ sumNative
result(uint256) := TMP_417(uint256)
RETURN result

#### Function: `mint`
<details><summary>View SlithIR Operations</summary>

```

_reserve1(uint112)= UNPACK TUPLE_1 index: 1 
TMP_138 = CONVERT token0 to IERC20
TMP_139 = CONVERT this to address
TMP_140(uint256) = HIGH_LEVEL_CALL, dest:TMP_138(IERC20), function:balanceOf, arguments:['TMP_139']  
balance0(uint256) := TMP_140(uint256)
TMP_141 = CONVERT token1 to IERC20
TMP_142 = CONVERT this to address
TMP_143(uint256) = HIGH_LEVEL_CALL, dest:TMP_141(IERC20), function:balanceOf, arguments:['TMP_142']  
balance1(uint256) := TMP_143(uint256)
TMP_144(uint256) = balance0 (c)- _reserve0

TMP_145(uint256) = balance1 (c)- _reserve1
amount1(uint256) := TMP_145(uint256)
TMP_146(bool) = INTERNAL_CALL, UniswapV2Pair._mintFee(uint112,uint112)(_reserve0,_reserve1)
feeOn(bool) := TMP_146(bool)
_totalSupply(uint256) := totalSupply(uint256)
TMP_147(bool) = _totalSupply == 0
CONDITION TMP_147
TMP_148(uint256) = amount0 (c)* amount1
TMP_149(uint256) = LIBRARY_CALL, dest:Math, function:Math.sqrt(uint256), arguments:['TMP_148']

## Contract: VaderPoolV2
### Linearised Constructor Execution sequence
1. `ERC721.constructor(string, string)`
2. `ReentrancyGuard.constructor()`
3. `BasePoolV2.constructor()`
4. `BasePoolV2.constructor(string, string)`
5. `BasePoolV2.constructor(IERC20)`
6. `Ownable.constructor()`

### Inter-Contract & Function Call Graph (Mermaid)
```mermaid
flowchart LR
    VaderPoolV2.mint --> VaderMath.calculateLiquidityUnits
    VaderPoolV2.mint --> SafeERC20.safeTransferFrom
    VaderPoolV2._burn --> SafeERC20.safeTransfer
    VaderPoolV2.doubleSwap --> VaderMath.calculateSwap
    VaderPoolV2.doubleSwap --> SafeERC20.safeTransfer
    VaderPoolV2.doubleSwap --> IERC20.balanceOf
    VaderPoolV2.swap --> VaderMath.calculateSwap
    VaderPoolV2.swap --> SafeERC20.safeTransfer
    VaderPoolV2.rescue --> SafeERC20.safeTransfer
    VaderPoolV2.rescue --> IERC20.balanceOf
    VaderPoolV2._update --> UQ112x112.encode
    VaderPoolV2._update --> UQ112x112.uqdiv
    VaderPoolV2.tokenURI --> Strings.toString
    VaderPoolV2._checkOnERC721Received --> Address.isContract
    VaderPoolV2._checkOnERC721Received --> IERC721Receiver.onERC721Received
    VaderPoolV2.mintSynth --> VaderMath.calculateSwap
    VaderPoolV2.mintSynth --> ISynth.mint
    VaderPoolV2.mintSynth --> ISynthFactory.createSynth
    VaderPoolV2.mintSynth --> ISynthFactory.synths
    VaderPoolV2.mintSynth --> SafeERC20.safeTransferFrom
    VaderPoolV2.burnSynth --> ISynthFactory.synths
    VaderPoolV2.burnSynth --> SafeERC20.safeTransfer
    VaderPoolV2.burnSynth --> SafeERC20.safeTransferFrom
    VaderPoolV2.burnSynth --> VaderMath.calculateSwap
    VaderPoolV2.burnSynth --> ISynth.burn
    VaderPoolV2.burn --> VaderMath.calculateLoss
    VaderPoolV2.mintFungible --> SafeERC20.safeTransferFrom
    VaderPoolV2.mintFungible --> VaderMath.calculateLiquidityUnits
    VaderPoolV2.mintFungible --> IERC20Extended.mint
    VaderPoolV2.mintFungible --> ILPWrapper.tokens
    VaderPoolV2.burnFungible --> ILPWrapper.tokens
    VaderPoolV2.burnFungible --> SafeERC20.safeTransfer
    VaderPoolV2.burnFungible --> IERC20Extended.burn
    VaderPoolV2.burnFungible --> SafeERC20.safeTransferFrom
    VaderPoolV2.setFungibleTokenSupport --> ILPWrapper.createWrapper
```
